(b) 40 mg/day: Antibody titers peak around Study Week 10-11. Total HAEs peak around Study Week 10-11 and then decline. Total HAEs generally decrease after Study Week 15.
L3128: Figure 1 illustrates exposure timelines for three subjects (Subject 1, Subject 2, and Subject 3) showing their first exposure, events, and last exposure times, used to calculate EAER and EAIR.
Subject 1: First exposure at Time = 0, event at Time = 3, last exposure at Time = 6. Exposure duration = 6-0=6.
Subject 2: First exposure at Time = 0, events at Time = 8, 9, 10, 11, last exposure at Time = 12. Exposure duration = 12-0=12.
Subject 3: First exposure at Time = 0, events at Time = 2, 3, 4, last exposure at Time = 5. Exposure duration = 5-0=5.
The calculations for EAER and EAIR are shown below:
EAER = \frac{\text{#of events}}{\text{Exposure time of subject 1} + \text{Exposure time of subject 2} + \text{Exposure time of subject 3}} = \frac{1+4+3}{6+12+5} = \mathbf{0.35 \text{ Events/Person-Year}}EAIR = \frac{\text{#of subjects with event}}{\text{Time to event of subject 1} + \text{Time to event of subject 2} + \text{Time to event of subject 3}} = \frac{1+1+1}{3+8+2} = \mathbf{0.23 \text{ Events/Person-Year}}
L3129: Figure 2 is a line graph showing the Mean Cumulative Function (MCF) of All Events (AEs) over time (Days) for two arms: 20 mg/day (red line) and 40 mg/day (blue line). The Y-axis represents the MCF (# of events), ranging from 0 to 20. The X-axis represents Time (Days), ranging from 0 to 1500. Both curves start at 0 and increase rapidly, leveling off around 1000 days. The 20 mg/day arm consistently shows a higher MCF than the 40 mg/day arm, reaching approximately 18 events compared to 13 events at 1000 days.
Figure 2: Mean cumulative function of AEs by 20mg/day and 40mg/day in 165-301. The 20 mg/day arm shows a higher cumulative number of events than the 40 mg/day arm.
L3130: Figure 3 is a step function graph showing the Mean Cumulative Function (MCF) of FDA adjudicated anaphylaxis over time (Days) for two arms: 20 mg/day (red line) and 40 mg/day (blue line). The Y-axis represents the MCF (# of events), ranging from 0.00 to 0.09. The X-axis represents Time (Days), ranging from 0 to 1500. Both curves start at 0 and increase rapidly, leveling off around 1000 days. The 40 mg/day arm consistently shows a higher MCF than the 20 mg/day arm, reaching approximately 0.08 events compared to 0.07 events at 1000 days.
Figure 3: Mean cumulative function of FDA adjudicated anaphylaxis (165-301). The 40 mg/day arm shows a higher cumulative number of FDA adjudicated anaphylaxis events than the 20 mg/day arm.
L3131: Figure 1 displays two line graphs showing Mean Plasma Phenylalanine Levels (\mu\text{M}) versus Study Day (0 to 120) for ENU2 mice administered rAvPAL-PEG or Vehicle SC weekly for 16 weeks. The graphs are separated by sex: Females (top) and Males (bottom).
The legend defines four groups:
Group 1 (Control): Blue circles
Group 2 (80 \to 40 mg/kg): Green diamonds
Group 3 (80 \to 20 mg/kg): Red triangles
Group 4 (40 \to 20 mg/kg): Purple squares
The Y-axis ranges from 0 to 2500 \mu\text{M}. The X-axis represents Study Day (0 to 120).
Females: All groups show high variability in plasma phenylalanine levels. Group 1 (Control) generally maintains levels between 1500 and 2000 \mu\text{M}. Groups 2, 3, and 4 show significant fluctuations, often dropping below 500 \mu\text{M} and rising above 2000 \mu\text{M}. Group 2 (80 \to 40 mg/kg) and Group 3 (80 \to 20 mg/kg) generally exhibit higher peak levels compared to Group 4 (40 \to 20 mg/kg).
Males: Similar to females, all groups show high variability. Group 1 (Control) generally maintains levels between 1000 and 1500 \mu\text{M}. Groups 2, 3, and 4 show significant fluctuations, often dropping below 500 \mu\text{M} and rising above 1500 \mu\text{M}. Group 2 (80 \to 40 mg/kg) and Group 3 (80 \to 20 mg/kg) generally exhibit higher peak levels compared to Group 4 (40 \to 20 mg/kg).
L3132: Line graph showing Mean Plasma Phenylalanine levels (\mu\text{M}) versus Study Day for ENU2 mice administered PAL, SC on study days 1, 4, and 8. The X-axis represents Study Day (-2, Dose 2, Dose 6, Dose 10, 14). The Y-axis represents Phenylalanine (\mu\text{M}), ranging from 0 to 3000. Error bars are included for all data points.
Legend:
Vehicle (Pink line with squares)
AvPAL 0.2 IU/mouse (Green line with triangles)
AvPAL 1.0 IU/mouse (Blue line with inverted triangles)
AvPAL-PEG 0.2 IU/mouse (Purple line with diamonds)
AvPAL-PEG 1.0 IU/mouse (Red line with circles)
R91K-PEG 1.0 IU/mouse (Black line with asterisks)
The graph illustrates that Vehicle and AvPAL groups maintain high phenylalanine levels (around 1800-2000 \mu\text{M}). The R91K-PEG 1.0 IU/mouse group shows a significant reduction in phenylalanine levels, dropping to near 0 \mu\text{M} by Study Day 14. The AvPAL-PEG groups show intermediate effects, with AvPAL-PEG 1.0 IU/mouse showing a slight increase in phenylalanine levels towards Study Day 14.
L3133: Figure 3 is a line graph showing Group Mean Phe Levels (mM) on the Y-axis (ranging from 0.00 to 1.75) versus Days on Study (X-axis, ranging from -10 to 170) for ENU2 Male Mice administered three PAL variants (NpPAL PEG, AvPAL PEG, R91K PEG) or Vehicle via SC Injection Weekly. The legend indicates four groups: NpPAL PEG (Red line with squares), AvPAL PEG (Green line with triangles), R91K PEG (Blue line with circles), and Vehicle (Purple line with dots). Arrows indicate Dose Administration points, occurring approximately every 10 days starting from Day 10. The Vehicle group maintains Phe levels around 1.25 mM. The R91K PEG group shows significant fluctuations, often dropping below 0.5 mM after administration. The NpPAL PEG and AvPAL PEG groups generally maintain Phe levels below 0.5 mM after administration, although AvPAL PEG shows a sharp peak around Day 60 (approx. 1.0 mM).
L3134: Figure 4 is a line graph showing Group Mean Phe Levels (mM) on the Y-axis (ranging from 0.00 to 1.75) versus Days on Study (X-axis, ranging from 130 to 180) for ENU2 Male Mice receiving wtAvPAL-PEG in a dose de-escalation study. The legend indicates three dose groups: 4 IU/dose (Green line with triangles), 2 IU/dose (Red line with squares), and 1 IU/dose (Blue line with circles). Arrows indicate Dose Administration points. The 4 IU/dose group shows a sharp peak around Day 135 (approx. 1.5 mM). The 2 IU/dose group shows a peak around Day 155 (approx. 0.5 mM). The 1 IU/dose group shows a peak around Day 165 (approx. 0.25 mM). The 4 IU/dose group generally maintains Phe levels below 0.5 mM after administration, while the 2 IU/dose and 1 IU/dose groups show higher peaks.
L3135: Figure 5 is a line graph showing Group Mean Body Weights (Weight in g) versus Days in Study (from -7 to 49) for ENU2 mice administered PAL via weekly SC injection for 8 weeks. The graph compares Vehicle (Tris NaCl) and various doses of AvPAL C503/565S PEG and AvPAL PEG.
The legend identifies the groups:
Vehicle (Tris NaCl)
AvPAL C503/565S PEG (0.25 IU/mouse)
AvPAL C503/565S PEG (1.0 IU/mouse)
AvPAL C503/565S PEG (4.0 IU/mouse)
AvPAL PEG (4.0 IU/mouse)
The Vehicle group shows the highest weight, peaking around 38 g at Day 35. The AvPAL PEG (4.0 IU/mouse) group also peaks around 38 g at Day 35. The AvPAL C503/565S PEG groups (0.25, 1.0, and 4.0 IU/mouse) generally show lower weights, peaking around 35 g at Day 35. All treated groups show a significant weight gain compared to the initial weight (Day 0) and stabilize around 33-34 g by Day 49.
L3136: Figure 6 displays two line graphs showing Group Mean Plasma Phenylalanine (Phe) Levels (nM) versus Days in Study (-6 to 57) for ENU2 mice administered PAL via weekly subcutaneous (SC) injection for 8 weeks. The graphs are separated by sex: Males (top) and Females (bottom).
The Y-axis represents Phenylalanine level (nM), ranging from 0 to 3000. The X-axis represents Days in Study, marked at -6, 1, 8, 15, 22, 29, 36, 43, 50, and 57. Dose points are marked by 'X'.
The legend defines the treatment groups:
Vehicle (Tris NaCl) (Green line, marked with asterisks)
AvPAL C503/565S PEG (0.25 IU/mouse) (Pink line, marked with squares)
AvPAL C503/565S PEG (1.0 IU/mouse) (Red line, marked with triangles)
AvPAL C503/565S PEG (4.0 IU/mouse) (Blue line, marked with circles)
AvPAL PEG (4.0 IU/mouse) (Purple line, marked with diamonds)
Males Graph: The Vehicle group shows high baseline Phe levels (around 2000-2500 nM) that fluctuate significantly, peaking around 2500 nM at Day 22. All treated groups show a sharp decrease in Phe levels immediately after the first dose (Day 1), dropping close to 0 nM. Phe levels generally remain low or near 0 nM until Day 15, after which they rise sharply, often exceeding the Vehicle group levels, particularly for the 0.25 IU and 1.0 IU groups, which show peaks around 1500-1700 nM. The 4.0 IU group generally maintains lower levels than the 0.25 and 1.0 IU groups after Day 15.
Females Graph: The Vehicle group shows high baseline Phe levels (around 1800-2200 nM) that fluctuate significantly, peaking around 2500 nM at Day 50. All treated groups show a sharp decrease in Phe levels immediately after the first dose (Day 1), dropping close to 0 nM. Phe levels generally remain low or near 0 nM until Day 15, after which they rise sharply. The 0.25 IU group shows the highest peak levels, reaching around 2500 nM at Day 50. The 1.0 IU group peaks around 2000 nM at Day 50. The 4.0 IU group generally maintains lower levels than the 0.25 and 1.0 IU groups after Day 15.
L3137: Line graph showing Group Mean Body Weights (Weight in g) versus Time (Days) for ENU2 Mice administered PAL via Weekly SC Injection for 8 Weeks Plus 16 Week Extension. The X-axis ranges from -6 to 231 days. The Y-axis ranges from 25 to 50 g. Six groups are plotted: Tris-HCl Vehicle (open circles), Av PAL PEG 1:3 (open diamonds), AvPAL C503S/C565S PEG 1:1.16 (solid squares), AvPAL C503S/C565S PEG 1:2.4 (solid triangles), and AvPAL C503S/C565S PEG 1:3 (solid inverted triangles). All groups show weight gain over time. The Tris-HCl Vehicle group generally maintains the lowest weight, while the PEGylated PAL groups show higher and increasing weights, with the 1:1.16 and 1:2.4 variants showing the highest final weights.
L3138: Line graph showing Group Mean Plasma Phe Levels (mM) versus Time (Days) for ENU2 mice administered PAL via SC Weekly Injection for 8 Weeks. The Y-axis ranges from 0 to 2.5 mM, and the X-axis ranges from -6 to 57 days. The legend identifies the groups:
Tris-HCl Vehicle (Open circles)
Av PAL PEG 1:3 (Open diamonds)
rAvPAL PEG 1:1.16 (Solid squares)
rAvPAL PEG 1:2.4 (Solid triangles)
rAvPAL PEG 1:3 * (Open diamonds with asterisk)
Dose Point (Upward triangles)
The graph illustrates that the rAvPAL PEG 1:1.16 group shows the most significant and sustained reduction in plasma Phe levels, dropping below 0.2 mM after the initial dose point (Day 1) and remaining low throughout the 8-week study period. The Vehicle group shows high and fluctuating Phe levels. The rAvPAL PEG 1:2.4 group also shows a strong reduction, but the levels fluctuate more than the rAvPAL PEG 1:1.16 group. The rAvPAL PEG 1:3 * group shows a moderate reduction.
L3139: Line graph showing Group Mean Plasma Phenylalanine (Phe) Levels (mM) versus Time (Days) for ENU2 mice administered rAvPAL-PEG via weekly subcutaneous (SC) injection for a 16-week extension of dosing. The X-axis ranges from 128 to 226 days, marked every 14 days. The Y-axis ranges from 0 to 2.5 mM, marked every 0.5 mM.
Legend:
Tris-HCl Vehicle (Open circles)
rAvPAL PEG 1:3 * (Solid diamonds)
Dose Point (Triangles)
The Tris-HCl Vehicle group shows high and fluctuating Phe levels, starting around 1.4 mM at Day 128 and generally remaining between 1.0 and 1.5 mM throughout the study period (up to Day 226).
The rAvPAL PEG 1:3 * group shows significantly lower Phe levels, generally below 0.5 mM, with peaks reaching approximately 0.5 mM at Day 142 and Day 156. The dose points (triangles) are marked along the time axis, indicating the weekly injection schedule.
L3140: Figure 10 is a dual-axis line graph showing Mean (+SD) rAvPAL-PEG and Phenylalanine (Phe) Plasma Levels over 240 hours post-dose in Homozygote Male ENU2 Mice following a single 20-mg/kg subcutaneous injection of rAvPAL-PEG (Phase 1/2 Drug Product Comparison).
The X-axis represents Time (Hours Postdose) from 0 to 240.
The left Y-axis represents Plasma rAvPAL-PEG (\mug/mL), ranging from 0 to 400.
The right Y-axis represents Plasma Phe (mM), ranging from 0.0 to 2.0.
Four data series are plotted:
rAvPAL-PEG (Phase 2, Lot NP160706) (Solid line with filled circles)
rAvPAL-PEG (Phase 1, Lot AP160703) (Solid line with filled triangles)
Phenylalanine (Phase 2, Lot NP160706) (Dashed line with open circles)
Phenylalanine (Phase 1, Lot AP160703) (Dashed line with open triangles)
The rAvPAL-PEG levels (Phase 2 and Phase 1) show a sharp peak around 24 hours post-dose, reaching approximately 200 \mug/mL (Phase 2) and 220 \mug/mL (Phase 1). Both rAvPAL-PEG levels decline significantly by 48 hours and remain low thereafter, generally below 100 \mug/mL by 120 hours.
The Phenylalanine (Phe) levels (Phase 2 and Phase 1) show a rapid increase starting around 24 hours, reaching approximately 1.6 mM (Phase 2) and 1.0 mM (Phase 1) by 240 hours. Both Phe levels are significantly elevated compared to baseline (0.0 mM) throughout the 240-hour period.
